Application code reaches the database only through the shipped\n  persistence package. Money is integer cents at every layer; never floating point.\n\n## story database\nsummary:      Declare the accounts, categories, and transactions stores and every repository operation the ledger needs.\ntype:         foundational\nkind:         capability\nphase:        1\nimplements:   DATABASE.md\nstack:        python.md, persistence.md, sqlite.md\nprovides:     accounts.*, categories.*, transactions.*\ndepends:      foundation\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  Author DATABASE.md and let Drydock generate the persistence package. Write no persistence code.\n\n  Tables: accounts(account PK, name), categories(category PK, description), transactions(txn_id\n  INTEGER PK, date, account REFERENCES accounts, category REFERENCES categories, amount INTEGER\n  cents, memo). Declare one access pattern per repository operation any story needs: create, get,\n  update, delete, list for each table; per-account transaction count and balance sums; the maximum\n  txn_id; per-account list of transactions; and delete-all operations for each table so init can\n  empty the ledger.\n\n## story cli-foundation\nsummary:      Stand up the ledger executable, the cli package entry point, argument dispatch, and exit-code mapping.\ntype:         foundational\nkind:         capability\nphase:        1\nimplements:   FEATURE-CliFoundation.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger, cli.main, cli.testing.cli_runner\ndepends:      foundation, database\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  Create the executable ./ledger at the application root, the cli package with main(argv) -> int,\n  and cli/testing.py exposing cli_runner. Provide a command registry that later stories register\n  verbs into, and a single error type carrying an exit code (1 operational, 2 usage, 3 rejected,\n  4 not found). main writes nothing to stdout on failure and exactly one diagnostic line to stderr,\n  and returns the exit code. Success output is CSV with \\n line endings, no quoting.\n\n## story ledger-rules\nsummary:      Provide shared validation and money helpers for codes, names, memos, amounts, dates, and balance projection.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        1\nimplements:   FEATURE-LedgerRules.md\nstack:        python.md\nprovides:     ledger_rules.parse_amount, ledger_rules.format_amount, ledger_rules.validate_date, ledger_rules.validate_code, ledger_rules.validate_text, ledger_rules.check_balances\ndepends:      cli-foundation\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  Implement pure, driver-free helpers. Codes are 1-12 chars of A-Z0-9. Names and descriptions are\n  1-40 chars; memos 0-60 chars; none contain a comma or newline. Amounts on input match\n  -?DIGITS[.D[D]], never zero, parsed with integer arithmetic into cents. Output amounts have\n  exactly two decimals, a leading - when negative, no thousands separator. Dates are real calendar\n  dates YYYY-MM-DD. Violations raise the rejected error (exit 3).\n\n  Provide a balance projection helper that applies a proposed set of added, changed, and removed\n  transactions to current per-account balances and rejects when any resulting balance is below zero.\n\n## story db-location\nsummary:      Resolve the database location from DATABASE_URL, DATABASE_PATH, then data/app.db.\ntype:         service\nkind:         integration\nphase:        1\nimplements:   FEATURE-DatabaseLocation.md\nstack:        python.md\nprovides:     config.database_dsn\ncovers:       DB-001\ndepends:      cli-foundation\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  Implement a single config function returning the DSN: DATABASE_URL when set, else DATABASE_PATH,\n  else data/app.db. A sqlite:// prefix or bare path selects SQLite; postgres:// and postgresql://\n  values are recognised and passed to open_database unchanged. Create the parent directory of a\n  default SQLite file path when missing. Only this function reads these environment variables.\n\n## story storage-failure\nsummary:      Open the store for each command and exit 1 with a stderr diagnostic when storage is unreachable or tables are missing.\ntype:         service\nkind:         integration\nphase:        1\nimplements:   FEATURE-StorageFailure.md\nstack:        python.md\nprovides:     storage.open_ledger\ncovers:       DB-002\ndepends:      db-location\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  Provide the helper every database-touching command uses to open the store through\n  open_database(dsn) and verify the three tables exist through the persistence library. An\n  unreachable database or missing tables raises the operational error (exit 1), writes one stderr\n  line, and nothing to stdout. State written by one invocation is visible to the next.\n\n## story ledger-init\nsummary:      Implement ledger init, which prepares an empty ledger and removes existing records on rerun.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        1\nimplements:   FEATURE-LedgerInit.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger init\ncovers:       LEDGER-001\ndepends:      db-location\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  ledger init takes no arguments and writes nothing. It provisions the declared schema through the\n  persistence library when the tables are absent (a fresh database file must work), then deletes\n  every transaction, then every account and category, through the delete-all operations. Rerunning\n  empties a populated ledger. Exit 0.\n\n## story ledger-count\nsummary:      Implement ledger count, writing row counts for accounts, categories, and transactions.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        1\nimplements:   FEATURE-LedgerCount.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger count\ncovers:       LEDGER-002\naccepts:      st-004\ndepends:      ledger-init, storage-failure\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  Write the header table,rows then accounts, categories, transactions rows in that order, each\n  count read from the store. Take no arguments; a wrong argument count is a usage error (exit 2).\n\n## story load-accounts\nsummary:      Implement ledger load accounts, validating every CSV row before any write.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-LoadAccounts.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger load accounts\ncovers:       LOAD-001\naccepts:      st-007\ndepends:      ledger-rules, storage-failure, ledger-init\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  Read the CSV with the csv module. The header must equal account,name exactly, else exit 3 with no\n  write. A missing or unreadable file exits 1. Validate every row (code and name rules) and reject\n  duplicate codes within the file or already stored (exit 3) before the first write. Success writes\n  table,loaded then accounts,<rows>. A header-only file loads zero rows and exits 0.\n\n## story load-categories\nsummary:      Implement ledger load categories, validating every CSV row before any write.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-LoadCategories.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger load categories\ncovers:       LOAD-002\naccepts:      st-007\ndepends:      load-accounts\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  Same contract as load accounts for the categories table: header category,description, code and\n  description rules, duplicate codes rejected with exit 3, nothing written on any rejection.\n  Success writes table,loaded then categories,<rows>.\n\n## story load-transactions\nsummary:      Implement ledger load transactions, keeping file txn_ids and rejecting the whole file on any bad row or negative balance.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-LoadTransactions.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger load transactions\ncovers:       LOAD-003\naccepts:      st-004, st-007, st-008\ndepends:      load-categories\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  Header must equal txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo. Validate every row before any write:\n  integer txn_id (unique within the file and against stored rows), real date, amount rule, memo\n  rule, account and category exist. Project the resulting per-account balances over stored plus\n  loaded rows and reject (exit 3) if any is below zero. Keep each file txn_id. Success writes\n  table,loaded then transactions,<rows>. The seed_transactions_bad file is rejected on its unknown\n  account with none of its rows stored.\n\n## story account-add\nsummary:      Implement ledger account add.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-AccountAdd.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger account add\ncovers:       ACC-001\naccepts:      st-006, st-007\ndepends:      ledger-rules, storage-failure\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  ledger account add <code> <name> validates code and name, rejects a duplicate code with exit 3\n  and no write, then writes the row account,name,transactions,balance for the new account\n  (0 transactions, balance 0.00). Introduce the account verb group dispatch that later account\n  stories extend.\n\n## story account-get\nsummary:      Implement ledger account get.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-AccountGet.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger account get\ncovers:       ACC-002\naccepts:      st-006\ndepends:      account-add\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  Validate the code first (malformed exits 3 before any lookup). An absent well-formed code exits\n  4. Otherwise delete it and write deleted then the code on the next line,\n  with no header.\n\n## story txn-post\nsummary:      Implement ledger txn post.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-TxnPost.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger txn post\ncovers:       TXN-001\naccepts:      st-006, st-007, st-008\ndepends:      account-add, load-categories\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  ledger txn post <account> <category> <amount> <date> [<memo>]. Validate every input, confirm the\n  account and category exist, and reject any posting that leaves the account balance below zero\n  (all exit 3, nothing written). The new id is one more than the largest txn_id, or 1 when the\n  table is empty. Write txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo and the new row. An omitted memo\n  is the empty string.\n\n## story txn-get\nsummary:      Implement ledger txn get.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-TxnGet.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger txn get\ncovers:       TXN-002\naccepts:      st-006\ndepends:      txn-post\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  A non-integer id exits 3 before any lookup; an absent integer id exits 4. Otherwise write the\n  header and the stored row with a two-decimal amount.\n\n## story txn-amend\nsummary:      Implement ledger txn amend.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-TxnAmend.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger txn amend\ncovers:       TXN-003\naccepts:      st-006, st-007, st-008\ndepends:      txn-post\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  Validate the id (exit 3) and amount rule (exit 3), exit 4 for an absent id, and reject (exit 3,\n  nothing changed) an amendment that leaves any balance below zero. Change only the amount and\n  write the updated row.\n\n## story txn-delete\nsummary:      Implement ledger txn delete.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-TxnDelete.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger txn delete\ncovers:       TXN-004\naccepts:      st-006, st-007, st-008\ndepends:      txn-post\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  Validate the id (exit 3), exit 4 for an absent id, and reject (exit 3, nothing changed) a\n  deletion that leaves any balance below zero. Otherwise delete and write deleted then the id on\n  the next line.\n\n## story transfer-record\nsummary:      Implement a valid ledger transfer that records an equal debit and credit in category TRANSFER.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-TransferRecord.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger transfer\ncovers:       XFER-001\naccepts:      st-010\ndepends:      txn-post\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  ledger transfer <from> <to> <amount> <date>. Outgoing row takes the next id with -amount, memo\n  \"transfer to <to>\"; incoming row takes the following id with +amount, memo \"transfer from\n  <from>\"; both in category TRANSFER. Write the header and both rows. Both balances update and the\n  total cash is unchanged. The TRANSFER category must exist in the store.\n\n## story transfer-refusal\nsummary:      Refuse invalid or overdrawing transfers, storing neither row.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-TransferRefusal.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger transfer rejection\ncovers:       XFER-002\naccepts:      st-007, st-008\ndepends:      transfer-record\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  Equal accounts, a non-positive amount, an unknown account, an invalid date, a missing TRANSFER\n  category, or a resulting negative balance on the source exits 3 with stdout empty and neither\n  row stored. All checks run before the first write.\n\n## story report-balances\nsummary:      Implement ledger report balances computed from stored data.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-ReportBalances.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger report balances\ncovers:       RPT-001\naccepts:      st-005\ndepends:      load-transactions\nacceptance:   yes\ninstructions: |\n  Write account,name,transactions,balance per account ordered by code, then TOTAL,,<count>,<sum>.\n  Sums use integer cents. An empty ledger writes the header and TOTAL,,0,0.00.
